# Result files in this package

Aggregate result JSONs only (no per-instance files, training logs, checkpoints or API caches). Paths are the same relative paths as in the full
repository; absolute paths are rewritten to repo-relative ones and log notes are removed; numbers are untouched.
The table/figure generators in `remark/scripts/` read `analysis/*.json`, `locks/*.json` and the `summary.json` files below. Per-row recomputation
(`make_paper_stats.py`, absolute numbers in `make_full_table.py`, log parsing in `n932_e3_compute.py`) needs re-evaluated per-instance files and
training logs, which are not shipped; the paper numbers are in `crs/analysis/paper_fig_stats.json`, `paper_full_table.json`, `paper_*_tables.json` and the paired-comparison files.
`crs/{val,test}/<run>/summary.json` is the evaluation summary of one checkpoint (R@k / N@k, in-catalog rate, parse statistics); `crs/locks/*.json` is the
validation-selected checkpoint (best step and validation curve) of a run.

## books/audit (1 files)

| file | used by |
|---|---|
| `books/audit/e9_recall.json` | make_paper_stats.py (paper_fig_stats.json; needs per-instance files) |
